# UNCONFIRMED marker inventory (courses and shared lessons)

Generated 2026-11-03 by a read-only scan of `curriculum/courses/**/*.json` and `curriculum/shared/**/*.json` (936 JSON files, none unparseable). Matches are case-insensitive for `unconfirmed` and `not confirmed`.

Files with at least one marker: 862. Distinct lesson ids: 862.

## Decisions and method

- **Learner-visible** = a marker inside a field a learner reads in the lesson: `instruction`, `procedure`, `workedExamples`, `equipmentAndPpe`, `commonErrors`, `safetyAndRegulatory`, `qualityStandard`, `documentationEvidence`, `summary`, `learningOutcomes`, `knowledgeChecks` (question and options), `referencesAtStart`/`referencesAtEnd`, `editableTemplates` prompts and guidance, `assessmentRubric`. The rubric sits in the lesson file, so it is counted as learner-visible; SCHEMA.md says assessor answers belong in assessment files.
- **Metadata** = `sourceReferences`, `officialReferences` (title, url, pageDate, covers), and any other internal field. No code in `apps/` or `scripts/` reads these fields, so they are treated as not shown to learners. `sourceReferences` often holds internal research paths and matrix codes.
- **Topic** is assigned by keyword rules, applied in order (asbestos, gas, electrical, plumbing, work at height, dangerous goods, pesticide and wildlife, GST and tax, food and health, pool, licence, other). The rules run first on the clause containing the marker, then on the whole string with URLs removed, then on the lesson title and path. Basis for each row is noted only in the scan, not here. Treat topic counts as approximate: a clause about one topic can mention another, and "other" covers general cleaning, business and app content.
- Quotes are the marker plus surrounding words, capped at 15 words. Field paths use array indices, for example `instruction[0].body`.
